- **odyssey**: Fully autonomous, time-boxed TODO-driven work loop, one level above /iterate. The session becomes an OVERSEER that maintains a repo-root TODO.md with frozen completion evals, dispatches worker agents item by item, has fresh critics judge every completion against the stated eval, and notifies the user on every finished TODO, failed eval, or eval revision. Usage - /odyssey [duration e.g. 2h | 90m | until=HH:MM, default 2h] [optional focus hint] ([alexgaoth/claude-iterate](https://github.com/alexgaoth/claude-iterate/tree/HEAD/skills/odyssey))

- **oezkisaoglu-linkedin-tipps**: Turn corporate expertise into credible LinkedIn posts, newsletters, and participation plans using Ilkay Özkisaoglu's public tips. Use for CEOs, corporate influencers, and B2B experts seeking professional visibility and trust. ([getedgehq/skills](https://github.com/getedgehq/skills/tree/HEAD/oezkisaoglu-linkedin-tipps) · ★2)
- **of**: v0.9.6. Alias for /orderfield. Use when /of, a field must be resumed, or a multi-slice disk brief. Load sibling SKILL.md. Not a second contract. Do not trigger for a harness name alone. ([pedroknigge/orderfield](https://github.com/pedroknigge/orderfield/tree/HEAD/of) · ★1)
- **of-doctor**: Diagnose and fix failing OpenFOAM v2412 runs - crashes (FOAM FATAL), divergence, bounding spam, MPI/decomposition errors, mesh-quality failures. Use when an OpenFOAM solver run fails, diverges, produces nan, or a case errors out, e.g. "my simpleFoam run diverged", "icoFoam crashed", "why does checkMesh fail". Input: case path (and optionally a specific log file). Produces root-cause diagnosis + minimal fix + optional rerun. If a custom-solver-specific diagnosis command exists for your project, prefer it for that solver. ([swtbkim/openfoam-claude-suite](https://github.com/swtbkim/openfoam-claude-suite/tree/HEAD/skills/of-doctor) · ★2)
- **of-loop-status**: Inspect OwnFramework Loop run state, approval binding, candidate identity, verdict, and adapter evidence without mutating protected state. ([william-london/ownframework-loop](https://github.com/william-london/ownframework-loop/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/of-loop-status))
- **of-post**: Post-process OpenFOAM v2412 results - residual/convergence plots, forces and force coefficients, y+, min/max, flow rates, line samples/profiles, probes, field statistics, VTK export for ParaView, screenshots, and a results report. Use when the user asks to analyze, plot, extract numbers from, visualize, or report on a completed (or running) OpenFOAM case. Also use to check status or monitor convergence of a running simulation. ([swtbkim/openfoam-claude-suite](https://github.com/swtbkim/openfoam-claude-suite/tree/HEAD/skills/of-post) · ★2)
- **of-setup**: Set up, install, or configure the OpenFOAM CFD Suite on this machine. Use on first run of the plugin, when the user says "of-setup", "set up the OpenFOAM suite", "adapt to this machine", or after an OpenFOAM upgrade or a plugin update. Detects the host mode (Windows+WSL or native Linux), locates the OpenFOAM installation, writes the machine config (of-env.sh), generates skills/of-sim/references/environment.md, patches allowed-tools, seeds the case registry, and offers a 1-minute validation benchmark.
